## EXT-0003 · Metrical irrationality results related to values of the Riemann zeta-function

- 原件。arXiv:1802.03946v1。PDF 物理页 1, 2, 11, 12, 14, 15, 16。SHA-256 6f0ff21275b0c347c0b6dc9665860b13906b12ca766a3f12587301aac70b36d9。
- 度量丢番图逼近与扰动级数。Theorems 3.2--3.4; Corollary 4.1。在正 Fourier 维测度下，若干含取整的阶乘级数对几乎处处参数无理；并给出 zeta 值及欧拉常数的分数部聚点型充分判据。
- 条件或证据待查。从有理性假设导出分数部趋零，再以文中 Theorem 2.1 的均匀分布结论矛盾；对欧拉常数仅为条件判据。 未逐项复核 Theorem 2.1 的外部来源和欧拉常数判据所需聚点条件；没有给出该条件的证明。
- 相关入口。[math-euler-constant-irrationality](../../../math-euler-constant-irrationality/SKILL.md)。
- 第四批定向筛选。adopt。The checked preprint gives a concrete factorial fractional-part criterion for gamma (Corollary 4.2) that is distinct from installed Pade, Laguerre, and Sondow routes. Its source proof supplies the rationality-to-zero reduction, but the required infinite accumulation condition remains open. 后续条件。To turn this conditional route into an irrationality proof, establish the stated accumulation alternative for the exact factorial sequence; finite computations do not suffice.
- 已采用方法。Add an exact finite evaluator for the conditional factorial fractional-part criterion for gamma; explicitly preserves that no infinite accumulation property and hence no gamma irrationality result is proved. [方法说明](../../../math-euler-constant-irrationality/references/hancl-kristensen-factorial-fractional-criterion.md)。

The added tail majorant is independently proved in the local reference note. 后续条件。No unverified preprint proof is used. - birational dynamics and transcendence。Main theorem; §§1–2。Constructs explicit dominant rational self-maps of P^2 over characteristic not two whose dynamical degree is transcendental under the paper's non-root-of-unity condition on a+b i.
- 版本与引用。The proof compares degrees of iterates through blowups/toric and birational dynamics; the transcendence criterion is specific to the constructed map. Formal result checked; no general dynamical-degree transcendence test extracted.
- 第六批定向筛选。citation。The published Acta Mathematica 225 (2020), 193-225 construction gives transcendental dynamical degrees only for its specially constructed birational maps and non-root-of-unity parameter condition. It is not a general degree-transcendence test. 定位。Main theorem and sections 1-2, physical pp. 1, 2, 5, 20。全卷 33 页。 后续条件。A concrete use must reproduce the blowup/toric construction, degree-iterate recurrence, and check the parameter's non-root-of-unity condition.

## EXT-0590 · A New Proof of Nishioka's Theorem in Mahler's Method

- 原件。HAL hal-03830022v1 (27 Oct 2022); published C. R. Mathématique 361 (2023), 1011–1028。PDF 物理页 1, 3, 4, 18, 19, 20。SHA-256 0c9ea266634f6535d86568c89b1786477d92f347a8947b7b22a64619bd981935。
- Mahler-function relation lifting。Theorems 1.1, 1.2, 2.1; proof §§3–4。For a q-Mahler system at a regular algebraic α with 0<|α|<1, algebraic relations among specialized values lift to homogeneous Q[z]-relations; consequently specialization preserves the stated transcendence degree.
- 构件候选。The proof first lifts linear relations using relation matrices supplied by Hilbert's Nullstellensatz, lifts homogeneous relations via tensor powers, and then compares Hilbert-function growth. Regularity along α^{q^k} is essential. Candidate relation-lifting guardrail only: applicability requires an explicitly verified q-Mahler system, algebraic α in the open disk, and absence of singularities along its orbit.
- 本轮筛选。adopted_published_verified。Local pp. 1-4 checked for statements and opening proof plan. C. R. Math publisher record verifies Adamczewski-Faverjon, volume 361 (2023), pp. 1011-1028, DOI 10.5802/crmath.458. Adopted solely as a global regular-orbit guardrail.
- 已采用方法。Published-version audit against the C. R. Math. publisher record. Relation lifting is an external global admission condition for value assertions: algebraic point in the open disk and a regular full orbit. [方法说明](../../../math-hankel-pade-certificates/references/mahler-function-transcendence-admission.md)。
